About the Team Are you an analytical person who is deeply interested in AI and LLM technology? Is your superpower accurately categorizing information and identifying patterns? Do you love to solve interesting challenges and make a direct impact through your work? Join our Tel Aviv team as an AI Data Quality Analyst and help shape the future of AI. We're looking for analytical minds to contribute to our cutting-edge Large Language Model (LLM) project. In this unique role, you'll be performing LLM data quality tasks, ensuring consistency and quality in our conversations and helping design and support our flagship products. About the Role As an AI Data Quality Analyst, you will evaluate LLM-generated conversations and other text-based data, identify trends and regressions, and partner with AI Engineers and Product Managers to improve model performance. In this role, you will: Review and evaluate LLM-generated conversations and other text-based outputs against task-specific quality criteria and success metrics. Perform high-quality annotation and data labeling to create reliable datasets for model training, benchmarking, and evaluation. Monitor real production data to identify quality trends, regressions, and areas for improvement. Identify qualitative patterns, recurring failure modes, regressions, and opportunities to improve model behavior and user experience. Analyze evaluation results and clearly communicate insights, risks, and recommendations to cross-functional stakeholders. Work closely with our AI Engineers and Product Managers to understand the data needs and ensure data quality processes align with goals, ensuring Paradox and Workday products remain top in class. Strong attention to detail, sound judgment, and a high bar for consistency and quality. Creative, out-of-the-box thinking and a proactive approach to investigating ambiguous or unexpected model behavior. Technical curiosity and confidence working across web-based tools, labeling platforms, dashboards, and evolving internal interfaces. Day to day

  • Investigating customer activity (business and/or personal), using transaction monitoring systems to identify red-flag patterns, such as structuring, layering, and unusual cross-border flows
  • Conducting deep-dive research using open-source intelligence (OSINT) and adverse media tools to assess the reputational and FinCrime risk associated with high-risk individuals and businesses
  • Validating the legitimacy of wealth and fund origins by analysing documentation to ensure they align with the customer’s known profile
  • Drafting high-quality unusual activity reports
  • Providing guidance on high-risk onboarding decisions and recommending client relationships exits when activity falls outside risk appetite
  • Supporting other teams with front-line compliance expertise
  • Contributing to the calibration of monitoring rules and threshold settings to reduce false positives while ensuring the detection of evolving criminal typologies

כבר לא מקבלים בקשות About The Role Our Financial Crime team blends regulatory expertise with data-driven thinking to make sure our products meet legal and policy requirements and deliver real value to customers. In a fast-moving, digital environment, they stay one step ahead by finding smart, scalable ways to prevent financial crime. We're looking for a Financial Crime Analyst to manage our enhanced AML due diligence processes. You'll analyse complex financial crime cases, manage risks, and make independent decisions that impact Revolut’s risk profile. To support our 24/7 coverage, this role may include night and weekend shifts, which are fully compensated. Up to shape what's next in finance? Let's get in touch. What You'll Be Doing Investigating customer activity (business and/or personal), using transaction monitoring systems to identify red-flag patterns, such as structuring, layering, and unusual cross-border flows Conducting deep-dive research using open-source intelligence (OSINT) and adverse media tools to assess the reputational and FinCrime risk associated with high-risk individuals and businesses Validating the legitimacy of wealth and fund origins by analysing documentation to ensure they align with the customer’s known profile Drafting high-quality unusual activity reports Providing guidance on high-risk onboarding decisions and recommending client relationships exits when activity falls outside risk appetite Supporting other teams with front-line compliance expertise Contributing to the calibration of monitoring rules and threshold settings to reduce false positives while ensuring the detection of evolving criminal typologies What You'll Need A bachelor's degree (must be able to provide a certificate) Fluency in English and Hebrew (C1+ level) 2+ years of FinCrime experience, or 1+ years of experience in AML, FIU, or similar complex investigation fields where a risk-based approach is used An understanding of financial crime red flags and typologies, and how to identify them Experience redacting and submitting suspicious activity reports Familiarity with investigative methodologies and analytical tools (e.g., spreadsheets, filters, pivots, network charts) A willingness to work a variety of shifts, including nights and weekends Critical thinking skills and impeccable attention to detail The ability to multitask, prioritise, and work well under pressure Nice to have A degree or equivalent in law or a STEM subject An industry-recognised certification, such as ICA or ACAMS Fluency in other languages Building a global financial super app isn’t enough. כבר לא מקבלים בקשות Healthcare Data Analyst About the Company Antidote Health is a tech- driven health insurance company on a mission to make healthcare easy, accessible, and more affordable. We’re rethinking how healthcare works—using technology to remove the friction, complexity, and delays that define the traditional system. Instead of navigating paperwork, long waits, and disconnected experiences, we’re building a simpler, more connected way for people to get care. Our platform combines health coverage, virtual care, and a modern member experience into one seamless system designed to help people actually use their benefits. We offer Affordable Care Act (ACA) health plans in Arizona and Ohio, and we’re growing quickly. We’re building a company focused on solving real problems—with real impact on people’s lives. Position Overview We are looking for an experienced Healthcare Data Analyst to join our Data department and help turn complex healthcare data into clear, actionable insights that improve business performance, member experience, operational efficiency, and healthcare outcomes. The ideal candidate combines strong analytical skills with curiosity about how healthcare works, together with the ability to communicate insights clearly and effectively in both English and Hebrew, verbally and in writing. This includes explaining the story behind the numbers, translating analysis into clear and actionable messages, and supporting stakeholder decision-making through discussions, reports, and presentations as needed. You will work closely with business, medical, operations, finance, product, and technology teams, using data from areas such as Healthplan M embership, Medical Claims, Providers, medical Utilization, and customer interactions . A successful Healthcare Data Analyst does more than produce reports. You understand the business question behind the request, determine which data can answer it, challenge questionable assumptions, validate the results, and turn the analysis into a clear recommendation or decision. You will help us build a culture where data is trusted, understood, and used to drive action . Key Responsibilities Analyze healthcare data to identify trends, opportunities, risks, and operational issues. Develop and maintain dashboards, reports, and KPIs used by executive stakeholders. Work with claims, enrollment, member, provider, utilization, and financial data. Partner with stakeholders to understand their needs and help them translate business questions into data analysis that supports decisions. Perform ad-hoc analyses supporting strategic and operational decisions. Validate data quality and identify inconsistencies, missing data, or potential data pipeline issues. Work with data engineering and analytics teams to improve data models and reporting infrastructure. Support regulatory, operational, and management reporting when needed. Present findings clearly to both technical and non-technical audiences. Qualifications Required: 5+ years of experience in data analysis, healthcare intelligence, or a similar analytical role. Strong Python and SQL skills and experience working with large datasets. Experience with BI and visualization tools such as Looker, Tableau or similar. Experience with cloud data platforms such as BigQuery, Snowflake, or Redshift. Strong analytical thinking and ability to break ambiguous business questions into structured analyses. Ability to communicate insights clearly and explain the story behind the numbers Strong attention to detail and commitment to data accuracy. Ability to work independently while collaborating effectively with multiple business functions. Master’s degree (with thesis) in a quantitative field such as Neurobiology, Bioinformatics, Statistics, Economics, Mathematics, Industrial Engineering, Public Health, or a related discipline. Healthcare Experience- preferred: Relevant experience may include working with: health insurance or payer data, medical claims and utilization, healthcare operations, clinical or population-health data. Experience with concepts such as ICD, CPT/HCPCS, NPI, taxonomy, claims adjudication, prior authorization, risk adjustment, or healthcare quality measures is an advantage. Preferred: Experience building or working with semantic layers and standardized metric definitions. Familiarity with US healthcare regulations and payer operations. Experience working in a fast-growing or highly cross-functional organization. Experience using AI tools to accelerate analytics, investigation, or reporting workflows. Equal Opportunity Statement Antidote Health is an equal opportunity employer and makes employment decisions without regard to race, color, religion, sex, age, disability, marital status, pregnancy, sexual orientation, gender identity, national origin, military reserve duty, or any other protected status under applicable law.

What you’ll need

  • Required
  • 5+ years of experience in data analysis, healthcare intelligence, or a similar analytical role
  • Strong Python and SQL skills and experience working with large datasets
  • Experience with BI and visualization tools such as Looker, Tableau or similar
  • Experience with cloud data platforms such as BigQuery, Snowflake, or Redshift
  • Strong analytical thinking and ability to break ambiguous business questions into structured analyses
  • Ability to communicate insights clearly and explain the story behind the numbers
  • Strong attention to detail and commitment to data accuracy
  • Ability to work independently while collaborating effectively with multiple business functions
  • Master’s degree (with thesis) in a quantitative field such as Neurobiology, Bioinformatics, Statistics, Economics, Mathematics, Industrial Engineering, Public Health, or a related discipline

  • Relevant experience may include working with: health insurance or payer data, medical claims and utilization, healthcare operations, clinical or population-health data. Day to day

  • Design, build, and improve machine learning models and graph/statistical algorithms — including clustering, anomaly detection, and time-series modeling and forecasting — to drive automated network optimization
  • Build a real, repeatable experimentation and model-deployment workflow (e.g., using MLflow or comparable tooling), taking models from notebook to production
  • Work directly with the near-real-time engineering team to identify where today's rule-based, threshold-driven decisions can be replaced by learned models that adapt to real network conditions
  • Mine large-scale time-series and topology data (stored in Elasticsearch, InfluxDB, and MongoDB) to uncover patterns in network behavior at the scale of thousands of cells and large user populations
  • Define and track quantitative success metrics so every model shipped can be proven to actually improve network outcomes, not just deployed and forgotten
  • Present findings and roadmap recommendations to engineering and business leadership

Parallel Wireless is reimagining mobile networks with innovative, energy-efficient Open RAN solutions. Join us as we lead the future of telecommunications, driving innovation through green and sustainable networks . Learn more about our mission, vision and values . We're hiring a Senior Data Scientist to own the non-real-time, ML-driven side of that system: turning large-scale network telemetry into models and analysis that make the entire platform smarter over time. You'll work directly on the algorithms that cluster cells, detect conflicts, and forecast load — and you'll partner closely with the real-time engineering team to push more of that intelligence into automated decision-making. Parallel Wireless is expanding the ecosystem for Open RAN with the GreenRAN™ energy-efficient Hardware-Agnostic technology. Deployed worldwide, our comprehensive 2G/3G/4G/5G Macro RAN solutions enhance network security while reducing operating expenses. As pioneers of Open RAN, we prioritize innovation, flexibility, and sustainability to help build a more connected, and green networks. Headquartered in the USA with global R&D centers, we are proud to serve over 60 customers worldwide and have been recognized with over 100 industry awards. Our mission is to accelerate GSMA’s Mobile Net Zero initiative by reducing TCO and driving innovation across the telecom ecosystem.Learn more at www.parallelwireless.com . Parallel Wireless embraces diversity and equality of opportunity. We are committed to building inclusive and diverse teams representing all backgrounds, with a wide range of perspectives, and empowering industry-leading skills. We welcome and consider applications to join our team from all qualified candidates, regardless of their characteristics. We comply with all applicable laws and regulations on non-discrimination in employment (and recruitment), as well as work authorization and employment eligibility verification requirements.Parallel Wireless does not accept unsolicited resumes or applications from agencies or individuals. Please do not forward resumes to our jobs alias, Parallel Wireless employees, or any other company location. Parallel Wireless is not responsible for any fees related to unsolicited resumes/applications. What You'll Do: Design, build, and improve machine learning models and graph/statistical algorithms — including clustering, anomaly detection, and time-series modeling and forecasting — to drive automated network optimization. . Build a real, repeatable experimentation and model-deployment workflow (e.g., using MLflow or comparable tooling), taking models from notebook to production. Work directly with the near-real-time engineering team to identify where today's rule-based, threshold-driven decisions can be replaced by learned models that adapt to real network conditions. Mine large-scale time-series and topology data (stored in Elasticsearch, InfluxDB, and MongoDB) to uncover patterns in network behavior at the scale of thousands of cells and large user populations. Define and track quantitative success metrics so every model shipped can be proven to actually improve network outcomes, not just deployed and forgotten. Present findings and roadmap recommendations to engineering and business leadership. What We're Looking For: 5+ years of applied data science / machine learning experience, including graph algorithms, clustering, anomaly detection, or time-series modeling. Strong Python skills; comfort working alongside Go-based production services. Experience with large-scale time-series and document data stores (Elasticsearch, InfluxDB, MongoDB, or comparable). Experience with ML experiment tracking and deployment tooling (MLflow or equivalent) and with streaming data pipelines (Kafka or comparable). Excellent communication skills — the ability to turn open-ended "why is the network behaving this way" questions into a shipped, measurable model. Outstanding B.Sc . graduates in these fields may also be considered. M.Sc . in Electrical Engineering, Computer Science, or Software Engineering. Nice to Have: Background in telecom, RF, or wireless networking (handovers, KPIs such as RSRP or PRB utilization, cell topology). Experience turning a hand-tuned, rule-based system into a learned model running in a live production environment. Experience with distributed or streaming compute frameworks.

Responsibilities As a Product Analyst, your impact will be: Lead the Product Data Domain and Culture: Take full ownership of product data analytics, initiating new projects, and radiating enthusiasm for data-driven decisions across the company. Define Product Metrics & Value: Partner closely with Product Managers to connect every feature, capability, or module to metrics that accurately reflect customer value and tie directly to our product’s North Star metric. Gather the Data: Extract, manipulate, and democratize data to ensure the foundation for our product metrics is robust and scalable, including hands-on data pipeline design and development. Empower Product Managers: Build tools, infrastructure, and dashboards to enable PMs to analyze data independently, with opportunities to define and shape our BI stack alongside our AI PM workspace. Hands-On Journey Mapping: Lead end-to-end analyses to optimize the overall user experience, identifying friction points, and illuminating "blind spots" in our metrics. Requirements What you need to succeed in this role: 4+ years of experience in Data Analysis with a proven track record specifically as a Product Analyst or in Product Data Analytics, working directly with Product Managers. Solid product sense with the ability to translate product vision and features into actionable adoption and value metrics. Deep technical expertise in SQL and Python for advanced data analysis and data modeling. Hands-on experience building dashboards and analytical tools. Exceptional analytical thinking to identify trends, uncover friction points in user journeys, and translate complex data into clear business and product impact. Self-driven and proactive attitude, ready to own the domain, initiate processes, and inspire cross-functional teams. Advantage: Hands-on experience with data analytics platforms (e.g., Databricks, Snowflakes, etc). Advantage: Experience with event-based product analytics platforms (e.g., Mixpanel, Amplitude, FullStory, etc). Advantage: B.Sc. or B.A. in a quantitative field (Computer Science, Statistics, Mathematics, Engineering, etc). About Claroty Claroty empowers organizations to protect the mission-critical infrastructure that underpins modern life. The AI-powered Claroty Platform serves as the single source of operational truth, providing the deepest visibility and broadest protection across cyber-physical systems (CPS), leveraging five core solutions: asset inventory, exposure management, network protection, secure access, and threat detection. Claroty helps organizations operationalize CPS protection through a programmatic approach designed to reduce risk, maintain operational integrity, and meet compliance–whether in the cloud with Claroty xDome or on-premise with Claroty Continuous Threat Detection (CTD). Claroty is deployed by hundreds of organizations at thousands of sites globally. The company is headquartered in New York City and has a presence in Europe, Asia-Pacific, and Latin America. To learn more, visit claroty.com. Claroty is an equal-opportunity employer committed to fostering a diverse and inclusive work environment for all. We encourage applications from candidates of ALL diverse backgrounds, and special accommodations are available upon request in all selection phases. The Role We're looking for a Head of Data to lead our Tel Aviv-based Data organization — spanning Data Infrastructure, Data Applications, and Data Adapters/Integration. This is a group-lead role: you will own the org's technical direction and delivery end to end, manage a group of Tech Leads and Staff Engineers (not just individual contributors), and be the single accountable owner for how Solidus ingests, stores, serves, and scales data across billions of events a day. This is a hands-on leadership role. You'll spend real time in code, architecture reviews, and incident debugging — not just in planning meetings — and you'll be expected to be physically present and available as the standard for how the org operates. What you'll own Org leadership — manage and grow the Tech Leads and Staff Engineers running Data Infrastructure, Data Applications, and Data Adapters; set technical direction, hiring plans, and career paths for the group. Scale and reliability — guide decisions on our ClickHouse, Kafka, Airflow, and K8s-based pipelines processing billions of events a day across real-time and T+1 batch workloads; own migrations and technical-debt paydown (e.g., decommissioning legacy systems) with a concrete, tracked plan. End-to-end technical accountability — own architecture and detailed design decisions across the data stack; personally review critical PRs and lead debugging on high-severity production issues rather than delegating and disengaging. Delivery and quality — drive sprint delivery, on-call health, testing rigor, and code-review discipline across all three teams; be the last line of defense against regressions and breaking changes reaching production. Cross-functional and external representation — partner with Product, Compliance, Customer Success, and clients on integrations and data quality; represent the Data org professionally and constructively in stakeholder-facing conversations, including under pressure. Team development — mentor and coach Tech Leads and engineers, give and receive feedback directly and without defensiveness, and build clear upward paths for high performers. Requirements: What we're looking for 8+ years of backend/data engineering experience, including 3+ years managing multiple teams or leading through Tech Leads/Staff Engineers (not just individual contributors). Genuinely hands-on: Read and review code, help debug production issues personally, and go deep on a system rather than relying on secondhand summaries. Experience with high-throughput, low-latency data systems at scale: streaming (Kafka or similar), analytical stores (ClickHouse, Snowflake, or similar), orchestration (Airflow), and containerized microservices (K8s). Strong SQL and query optimization experience; comfort owning schema versioning, data governance, and deduplication strategy at scale. Proven track record of end-to-end ownership — architecture through implementation through operational health — with a demonstrated habit of catching issues (code review, testing, monitoring) before they reach production. Experience with monitoring and observability tooling (Prometheus, Grafana, Datadog, or similar) and owning operational SLAs. Demonstrated reliability and availability as a leadership default — consistent, predictable presence; transparent, advance planning of time off/travel rather than after-the-fact notice. Clear, professional written and verbal communication with both engineering and non-engineering/external stakeholders, including a demonstrated pattern of incorporating critical feedback without defensiveness. AI fist: coding tools/harnesses as part of a modern engineering workflow. Domain experience in fintech, trading, or crypto compliance/surveillance. Experience integrating third-party/customer data sources or leading vendor-facing technical relationships. Tech Stack Java, Kafka, ClickHouse, Snowflake, Apache Airflow, Kubernetes, Redis, AWS, Prometheus/Grafana. Why this role You'll build and run a multi-team data organization from a position of real technical ownership — not managing status updates, but setting direction for the systems that process over a trillion events a day, with a mandate to raise the bar on rigor, reliability, and how the org shows up for its stakeholders.

What you’ll need

  • 8+ years of backend/data engineering experience, including 3+ years managing multiple teams or leading through Tech Leads/Staff Engineers (not just individual contributors)
  • Genuinely hands-on: Read and review code, help debug production issues personally, and go deep on a system rather than relying on secondhand summaries
  • Experience with high-throughput, low-latency data systems at scale: streaming (Kafka or similar), analytical stores (ClickHouse, Snowflake, or similar), orchestration (Airflow), and containerized microservices (K8s)
  • Strong SQL and query optimization experience; comfort owning schema versioning, data governance, and deduplication strategy at scale
  • Proven track record of end-to-end ownership — architecture through implementation through operational health — with a demonstrated habit of catching issues (code review, testing, monitoring) before they reach production
  • Experience with monitoring and observability tooling (Prometheus, Grafana, Datadog, or similar) and owning operational SLAs
  • AI fist: coding tools/harnesses as part of a modern engineering workflow
  • Domain experience in fintech, trading, or crypto compliance/surveillance
  • Experience integrating third-party/customer data sources or leading vendor-facing technical relationships

Description Job Summary We are seeking a highly skilled Senior Static Timing Analysis (STA) Engineer to join our ASIC implementation team. The ideal candidate will have extensive experience in timing analysis, timing closure, and signoff for complex SoC designs at advanced technology nodes. The engineer will work closely with RTL, Physical Design, DFT, and CAD teams to ensure successful timing convergence and tape-out. Key Responsibilities Own full-chip and block-level timing closure from synthesis through signoff. Perform static timing analysis across multiple operating modes and PVT corners. Develop, review, and maintain timing constraints (SDC). Analyze and resolve setup, hold, recovery, removal, clock gating, pulse width, and timing exception violations. Perform SI-aware timing analysis and crosstalk delay/noise debugging. Collaborate with Physical Design teams to optimize placement, CTS, routing, and ECO implementation for timing closure. Generate and validate timing ECOs while ensuring minimal impact on power, area, and functionality. Validate timing exceptions including false paths, multicycle paths, asynchronous paths, and generated clocks. Perform timing signoff using industry-standard EDA tools. Support timing correlation between implementation and signoff environments. Develop automation scripts to improve timing analysis and reporting. Participate in design reviews and provide technical guidance on timing-related issues. Mentor junior engineers and contribute to methodology improvements. Requirements Required Qualifications Bachelor's or Master's degree in Electrical Engineering, Computer Engineering, or a related field. Minimum 5 years of experience in ASIC Static Timing Analysis or Physical Design. Strong understanding of: Static Timing Analysis fundamentals Timing constraints (SDC) MCMM timing analysis Clock tree architecture and clock propagation Signal Integrity (SI) OCV, AOCV, POCV, and SOCV methodologies CRPR and advanced variation modeling Liberty (.lib), SPEF, SDF, and Verilog formats Experience with advanced technology nodes (16nm, 7nm, 5nm, 3nm, or similar). Strong debugging and root-cause analysis skills. Required Technical Skills Proficiency with STA and implementation tools such as: Synopsys PrimeTime Synopsys Fusion Cadence Innovus Cadence Tempus Synopsys StarRC Experience with Linux/Unix environments. Strong scripting skills in Tcl and Shell; familiarity with Perl or Python is preferred. Experience with version control systems such as Git. Preferred Qualifications Experience with hierarchical and full-chip timing signoff. Experience with timing signoff automation and methodology development. Understanding of synthesis, place-and-route, and physical implementation flows. Soft Skills Strong analytical and problem-solving abilities. Excellent communication and collaboration skills. Ability to work independently and manage multiple priorities. Attention to detail and commitment to design quality. Experience mentoring junior engineers and contributing to technical leadership. What You'll Bring Ownership of timing closure for complex ASIC or SoC designs. Ability to drive timing signoff independently while collaborating effectively across cross-functional teams. Expertise in advanced timing methodologies, SI-aware analysis, MCMM signoff, and ECO execution. A proactive mindset focused on continuous improvement, automation, and delivering successful tape-outs.

What you’ll need

  • Bachelor's or Master's degree in Electrical Engineering, Computer Engineering, or a related field
  • Minimum 5 years of experience in ASIC Static Timing Analysis or Physical Design
  • Strong understanding
  • Timing constraints (SDC)
  • MCMM timing analysis
  • Clock tree architecture and clock propagation
  • OCV, AOCV, POCV, and SOCV methodologies
  • CRPR and advanced variation modeling
  • Liberty (.lib), SPEF, SDF, and Verilog formats
  • Experience with advanced technology nodes (16nm, 7nm, 5nm, 3nm, or similar)
  • Strong debugging and root-cause analysis skills
  • Proficiency with STA and implementation tools such as
  • Experience with Linux/Unix environments
  • Strong scripting skills in Tcl and Shell
  • familiarity with Perl or Python plus

We're looking for a Senior Data Analyst who is both a pipeline builder and a data researcher. You'll own custom recurring feeds end to end — from methodology for new metrics, through pipeline design, to final acceptance before delivery. You'll lead conversations with internal stakeholders (US-based customer-facing teams and colleagues across R&D) and help generalize recurring logic into reusable models, metrics and packages the whole team builds on. Ideal for a rigorous, independent thinker looking for a delivery-oriented role where impact is tangible. • Responsibilities • Own custom feeds from requirements through delivery and ongoing maintenance • Develop methodologies and acceptance criteria for new metrics • Build reproducible, tested production pipelines in PySpark on Databricks , orchestrated with Airflow and Terraform • Partner with Solution Engineers and customer-facing teams on timelines, methodology and customer feedback • Build reusable components and packages; collaborate with R&D and Data Science to reuse existing datasets, models and products — and make your work reusable by others • Requirements • 5+ years in data analytics / data research with large-scale datasets • 3+ years hands-on Python and PySpark at scale • Solid applied statistics — sampling and estimation, bias and coverage, significance, uncertainty — and the ability to explain limitations to non-technical audiences • Demonstrated ownership of data quality : metrics and validations for datasets others depend on • Proficiency with AI-assisted coding (Claude Code, Cursor, Copilot), with judgment about when to trust, verify or discard output • Excellent written and spoken English ; comfortable presenting to customers • Ability to own deliverables independently across multiple teams under tight deadlines • B.Sc. in a quantitative field; M.Sc. / MBA or academic research experience — advantage • Technical Skills: PySpark (must, with performance awareness), Databricks or equivalent Spark platform, Airflow in production, Terraform / IaC, Git-based collaborative development • WHY JOIN PLACER.AI? • Join a rocketship! Compensation tells even better stories. We are looking for a curious, analytical, and highly motivated Compensation & Benefits Analyst to join our Global Compensation & Benefits team. This is an excellent opportunity for someone early in their career who loves working with data, enjoys solving puzzles, and wants to learn how global organizations make decisions about employee pay, benefits, rewards, and workforce strategy. You don't need to be a compensation expert yet - we will teach you that part. What we're really looking for is someone who gets excited about Excel formulas, asks "why?" more often than most people, and isn't afraid to dive into large datasets until they reveal their secrets. (Warning: prolonged exposure to this role may result in unexpectedly strong opinions about salary structures, market data, and pivot tables.) What You'll Do As part of our Global Compensation & Benefits team, you will: Compensation Analytics & Benchmarking Participate in salary benchmarking projects using leading market surveys and compensation databases. Analyze internal and external compensation data to support competitive and equitable pay decisions. Assist with the maintenance of salary ranges, job architecture, and compensation frameworks. Support annual compensation cycles, including merit reviews, promotions, and salary adjustments. Conduct compensation analyses and help prepare recommendations for managers and HR partners. Data, Reporting & Insights Partner with HR Operations on data management in the HR system (HRDB) - including uploads, audits, and ongoing maintenance of HR data - to safeguard data integrity across the employee lifecycle. Build and maintain reports, dashboards, and analyses using Excel, Power BI, and HR systems. Translate complex data into simple and actionable insights. Support pay equity, workforce planning, and regulatory reporting initiatives. Benefits & Total Rewards Assist in the evaluation and administration of employee benefits programs. Conduct market research and trend analysis on benefits and total rewards practices. Support communication efforts to help employees understand our rewards programs. Business Partnership Partner with HR, Talent Acquisition, Finance, Payroll, and business leaders on compensation-related initiatives. Contribute to projects that improve processes, governance, and the employee experience. Continuous Improvement Look for smarter, faster, and more efficient ways to work. Leverage AI and technology tools to enhance reporting, analyses, and decision-making. Bring fresh ideas and challenge existing assumptions. This is a temporary position for 3 months Qualifications Required Bachelor's degree in Economics, Statistics, Psychology, Business Administration, Human Resources, Industrial Engineering, or a related field. Strong analytical and problem-solving skills. Advanced Excel skills or a strong willingness to become an Excel wizard. High attention to detail and strong organizational skills. Excellent communication skills in English. Curiosity, ownership, and a growth mindset. Nice to Have Familiarity with compensation, benefits, finance, payroll, or HR analytics. Experience using AI tools to improve productivity and analysis. Basic knowledge of statistics and data visualization. Who Will Thrive in This Role? You might be a great fit if: You enjoy working with numbers as much as people. You get genuine satisfaction from finding an error that nobody else noticed. You ask thoughtful questions instead of accepting things at face value. You can explain a complicated analysis in language that normal humans understand. You view "I've never done that before" as an exciting opportunity rather than a problem. What You'll Learn This role offers a unique opportunity to build expertise in: Global compensation strategy Salary benchmarking and market intelligence Pay equity and regulatory compliance Executive and broad-based compensation programs Benefits design and governance Advanced people analytics Business partnering and stakeholder management You'll work closely with experienced Compensation & Benefits professionals and gain exposure to strategic projects that have a direct impact on thousands of employees worldwide. Final Note We're not looking for someone who already knows everything about Compensation & Benefits. We're looking for someone who is excited to learn everything about Compensation & Benefits. If you're naturally curious, analytical, driven, and ready to grow - we'd love to meet you. Bring your curiosity. We'll teach you the compensation part. Additional Information

כבר לא מקבלים בקשות THE ROLE We are looking for a Trading & Market Operations Analyst to support G Coin’s trading, treasury, and exchange operations across centralized and decentralized digital-asset exchanges. The role focuses on monitoring market conditions and liquidity, executing authorized transactions in accordance with internal policies, reconciling exchange and wallet balances, working closely with exchanges and external market makers, and identifying unusual market activity and operational risks. RESPONSIBILITIES Monitor G Coin activity across supported centralized and decentralized exchanges. Analyze trading volumes, spreads, order-book depth, liquidity, volatility, and market concentration. Execute authorized trading and treasury transactions within established limits and approval procedures. Monitor exchange and wallet balances and ensure accurate reconciliation of transactions and holdings. Coordinate operational matters with exchanges, external market makers, and other liquidity providers. Monitor and assess external market makers against agreed KPIs, service levels, contractual requirements, and market-integrity standards. Review market-maker reports and independently verify relevant trading and liquidity data. Identify and escalate unusual trading patterns, potential market abuse, operational anomalies, and liquidity risks. Prepare regular reports on market activity, liquidity conditions, balances, transactions, market-maker performance, and identified risks. Support exchange listings, trading-pair launches, wallet integrations, deposits, withdrawals, and related operational processes. Maintain complete records of transactions, approvals, communications, and account activity. Ensure that all activities comply with internal controls, exchange rules, applicable requirements, and market-integrity standards. REQUIREMENTS 1–3 years of experience in trading operations, treasury, financial-market analysis, brokerage, digital assets, or a related field. Practical understanding of cryptocurrency and digital-asset markets. Experience working with centralized and decentralized exchanges. Understanding of order books, liquidity, spreads, volatility, trading volume, and market risk. Strong analytical skills and confidence working with financial and market data. Proficiency in using AI tools for market research, data analysis, reporting, and workflow automation. High level of accuracy, integrity, responsibility, and discretion. Ability to follow defined authorization procedures and maintain clear transaction records. Strong written and spoken English. ADVANTAGES Experience working with or overseeing external market makers or liquidity providers. Experience with a digital-asset company, exchange, broker, fintech company, or treasury function. Familiarity with on-chain analytics, wallets, token bridges, and blockchain transactions. Experience identifying unusual market activity, potential manipulation, or compliance risks.

About Nebius: Nebius is leading a new era in cloud infrastructure for the global AI economy. We are building a full-stack AI cloud platform that supports developers and enterprises from data and model training through to production deployment, without the cost and complexity of building large in-house AI/ML infrastructure. Built by engineers, for engineers. From large-scale GPU orchestration to inference optimization, we own the hard problems across compute, storage, networking and applied AI. Listed on Nasdaq (NBIS) and headquartered in Amsterdam, we have a global footprint with R&D hubs across Europe, the UK, North America and Israel. Our team of 1,500+ includes hundreds of engineers with deep expertise across hardware, software and AI R&D. The role We are looking for a BI Developer to join the CISO Data Analytics team in Tel Aviv. This role is primarily focused on designing, developing, and maintaining Tableau dashboards and BI products that support decision-making across security operations, risk, compliance, and leadership. Approximately 60–70% of the role will focus on dashboard development, KPI design, data storytelling, and stakeholder engagement. What you'll do: Develop and deliver Tableau dashboards from requirements through deployment. Work directly with stakeholders to understand reporting needs and turn them into dashboards. Help translate business needs into KPIs and reporting metrics. Maintain and continuously improve existing dashboards, ensuring data quality and stakeholder adoption. Conduct ad hoc analyses and deep-dives to support business decisions. Write SQL queries to extract, join, and prepare data. Requirements Must have: 3–5 years of experience in a BI, analytics, or data visualisation role, with Tableau as your primary tool for most of that time. Advanced, hands-on Tableau skills: calculated fields, LOD expressions, parameters, table calculations, performance optimisation, and Tableau Server or Tableau Cloud. A portfolio of dashboards you built yourself - you should be able to speak in detail about design decisions, data sources and what each dashboard enabled. SQL: complex queries, joins, window functions and aggregations. Experience defining KPIs and metrics collaboratively with stakeholders, not just visualising numbers handed to you. Clear communicator, comfortable presenting findings to non-technical audiences. Strong written and verbal English. Security Domain – Strong Advantage Experience in a CISO, SOC, GRC, or security operations environment. Familiarity with security metrics - vulnerability management, incident response, or access control KPIs. Knowledge of frameworks such as ISO 27001, NIST CSF, or SOC 2. Experience working with security tooling data (SIEM, EDR, IAM, or ticketing platforms). Nice to have: Basic Python for analytics automation (not data engineering). Awareness of pipeline tools such as dbt or Airflow. Experience with cloud platforms (Azure, AWS, or GCP). What We Offer High-impact analytics role within a global cybersecurity organisation. Ownership of dashboards and BI products used by leadership. Opportunity to influence decision-making through data and analytics. Benefits & Perks: Competitive compensation Career growth and learning opportunities Flexibility and ownership Collaborative and innovative culture Opportunity to work on impactful AI projects International environment and talented teams What's it like to work at Nebius: Fast moving - Bold thinking - Constant growth - Meaningful impact - Trust and real ownership - Opportunity to shape the future of AI Equal Opportunity Statement: Nebius is an equal opportunity employer. We are committed to fostering an inclusive and diverse workplace and to providing equal employment opportunities in all aspects of employment. We do not discriminate on the basis of race, color, religion, sex (including pregnancy), national origin, ancestry, age, disability, genetic information, marital status, veteran status, sexual orientation, gender identity or expression, or any other characteristic protected by applicable law. Applicants must be authorized to work in the country in which they apply and will be required to provide proof of employment eligibility as a condition of hire. If you need accommodations during the application process, please let us know.

  • 3–5 years of experience in a BI, analytics, or data visualisation role, with Tableau as your primary tool for most of that time
  • Advanced, hands-on Tableau skills: calculated fields, LOD expressions, parameters, table calculations, performance optimisation, and Tableau Server or Tableau Cloud
  • SQL: complex queries, joins, window functions and aggregations

  • Strong understanding of data quality, governance, documentation, and the importance of consistent business definitions

About Nebius: Nebius is leading a new era in cloud infrastructure for the global AI economy. We are building a full-stack AI cloud platform that supports developers and enterprises from data and model training through to production deployment, without the cost and complexity of building large in-house AI/ML infrastructure. Built by engineers, for engineers. From large-scale GPU orchestration to inference optimization, we own the hard problems across compute, storage, networking and applied AI. Listed on Nasdaq (NBIS) and headquartered in Amsterdam, we have a global footprint with R&D hubs across Europe, the UK, North America and Israel. Our team of 1,500+ includes hundreds of engineers with deep expertise across hardware, software and AI R&D. The role Nebius is looking for a Head of Analytics. As Head of Analytics, you’ll lead the analytics team and strengthen how data is used to support decision-making across the company. Reporting to the VP of Data & Analytics, you’ll translate the broader data strategy into clear priorities, high-quality analytical delivery, and measurable business impact. You’ll manage and develop teams of analysts and BI, partner closely with senior stakeholders across Product, Sales, Marketing, Finance, Customer Success, and Operations, and ensure that the company has trusted metrics, actionable insights, and scalable analytical capabilities. This is a strategic and hands-on leadership role. You’ll be expected to set direction, create structure, raise analytical standards, and remain closely involved in the company’s most important business questions. You’re welcome to work in our offices in Tel Aviv, Israel Your responsibilities will include: Team leadership and development. Lead, mentor, and develop the analytics team. We've built the leading partnership automation platform that empowers brands to discover, engage, and convert their audiences at scale. From affiliate marketing to influencer collaborations, we help our clients build and manage profitable partnerships that drive real results. We're a team of passionate problem-solvers who are dedicated to helping our clients win in the ever-evolving world of digital marketing. • Why Join Us • We're looking for passionate, talented people who want to be part of a winning team. We also offer a competitive salary, generous benefits, and a flexible work environment that allows you to thrive both personally and professionally. If you're ready to grow your career and make a difference, we'd love to hear from you. • About Us • Konnecto, a Partnerize company, is the pioneer of Clickless Discovery. We empower the world’s most iconic brands to look beyond traditional analytics and understand the “dark” consumer journey — the critical path a user takes before they ever click. By leveraging massive behavioral datasets and sophisticated AI-driven agents, we provide actionable insights that help brands win at the earliest stages of consumer intent. As part of the Partnerize family — the global leader in partnership automation — our Tel Aviv team operates with the high-velocity, high-ownership mindset of a startup, backed by the stability and reach of a global enterprise. We are building the future of marketing intelligence, and we are doing it at a massive scale. • Role Mission • As our Product Analyst, you will play a key role in translating complex behavioral, marketing, and platform data into clear business insights that drive product strategy, customer value, and commercial decision-making. This is not a passive reporting role. We are looking for someone proactive, curious, and business-oriented — someone who can identify the right questions, investigate complex datasets, uncover meaningful patterns, and turn findings into actionable recommendations for Product, R&D, Customer Success, Sales, and leadership teams. Konnecto’s platform processes large-scale consumer journey data to help global brands and publishers understand influence, attribution, and performance in a clickless discovery environment. As our products become more sophisticated, we need a strong analytical partner who can connect data, product logic, customer needs, and business context into one clear story. Day to day

  • Leading a team of engineers, owning both its delivery and its development
  • Lead a mindset and process of continuous improvement within the team and meaningfully impact the team’s growth and success over time. Small, compounding improvements, delivered via fast iterations, are the operating rhythm we expect, not an aspiration
  • You are responsible for the professional well-being and career development of team members. Develop engineers through regular 1:1s, growth plans, performance reviews, and direct, candid feedback
  • Develop the next layer of leadership - tech leads, senior engineers ready to manage, and ICs ready to step into staff roles
  • Write code and review code. Be a credible voice in technical decisions because you have hands in the work, not because of your title - you are current, not rusty
  • Drive the team's architectural and technical design decisions in partnership with senior ICs, ensuring alignment on approach and implementation
  • Uphold engineering standards through thorough, constructive pull-request review and a healthy release process, keeping the team's components scalable and in a healthy state
  • Work AI-augmented and lead an AI-augmented team, applying sharp judgment about where AI helps and holding the same quality bar for what ships
  • Roadmap & delivery
  • Own the team's commitments - what was promised, when it was promised, and the quality bar that was held
  • Run a disciplined cadence of planning, prioritization, and delivery without becoming a process bureaucrat
  • Partner closely with the Technical Product Owner and Product team on scope, sequencing, and tradeoffs, maintaining a roadmap that reflects both near-term priorities and longer-term direction
  • Understand the broader business objectives behind the work, translate that context for the team, and ensure engineering effort clearly aligns with those objectives
  • React decisively to change - re-planning, re-prioritizing, and re-staffing as the business moves - while keeping the team engaged and steady through the shift
  • Own hiring for your team. Interview thoughtfully, decide quickly, and refuse to lower the bar to fill seats
  • Recruit, coach, and - when needed - make hard calls toward the standard the team is held
  • Operate as a role model for other engineering managers across the organization: share your operating model, mentor peers, and raise the management bench
  • Day-to-day communication & collaboration
  • Upwards: Communicate clearly and succinctly, giving leadership the right level of context and prioritizing escalations based on relevant, well-gathered information
  • Downwards: Empower senior engineers to own key areas of the product, and coach team members to stay focused, intentional, and aware of their impact
  • Sideways: Partner effectively with Product, Design, GTM, Customer Success, and other teams - raising challenges and opportunities constructively to support cross-team alignment

Description We're looking for a Data Analyst to join our core team and own the data, reporting, and insight layer that our biggest decisions are built on — what to greenlight, what to spend, and where to double down. You'll report into the Head of Data and work closely with Marketing, Product, and Content, but the core of the job is data work: querying, modeling, dashboarding, and rigorous analysis. As we scale, so will your scope — this is a launchpad role, not a fixed lane. Responsibilities Reporting & Dashboards: Own end-to-end reporting — build, maintain, and improve dashboards (Looker, Tableau, Power BI, or similar) — and ensure the pipelines feeding them are accurate, timely, and well-documented. Analysis & Insight Generation: Turn raw data into clear, actionable recommendations by analyzing funnels and attribution, tracking core KPIs (CAC, LTV, ROAS, retention), and proactively surfacing opportunities rather than waiting to be asked. Data Integration & Engineering: Define and validate tables and data models, define BI events and tracking specs with product and engineering, and work closely with the data engineering team to keep data clean, unified, and query-ready across analytics, ad/campaign, CRM, and product data sources. Experimentation: Support or run A/B tests across campaigns, product features, or content, applying statistical rigor to test design and readouts. Stakeholder Communication: Translate complex data into clear narratives for non-technical stakeholders, and present findings with a structured, problem-first point of view rather than just executing requests as asked. Requirements B.Sc. in Industrial Engineering/Economics/Statistics, or equivalent degree 4+ years in analytics, business intelligence, or a similar analytical role. Strong SQL and hands-on experience with BigQuery, Presto, or similar large-scale databases/warehouses. Hands-on experience with at least one BI tool (Looker, Tableau, Power BI, or similar). Experience building tables/data models and defining BI events, ideally in partnership with data engineering. Experience working with digital data sources (e.g., Google Analytics, ad platforms, CRM systems). Solid grasp of funnels, attribution modeling, and core performance KPIs. Able to communicate complex data clearly to non-technical stakeholders. High attention to detail; structured, problem-solving mindset. Huge Advantages Background in mobile / gaming / short-form content or other high-velocity B2C products. Experience designing and evaluating A/B tests, including statistical significance.

What you’ll need

  • B.Sc. in Industrial Engineering/Economics/Statistics, or equivalent degree
  • 4+ years in analytics, business intelligence, or a similar analytical role
  • Strong SQL and hands-on experience with BigQuery, Presto, or similar large-scale databases/warehouses
  • Hands-on experience with at least one BI tool (Looker, Tableau, Power BI, or similar)
  • Experience building tables/data models and defining BI events, ideally in partnership with data engineering
  • Experience working with digital data sources (e.g., Google Analytics, ad platforms, CRM systems)
  • Solid grasp of funnels, attribution modeling, and core performance KPIs
  • Able to communicate complex data clearly to non-technical stakeholders
  • High attention to detail; structured, problem-solving mindset
  • Background in mobile / gaming / short-form content or other high-velocity B2C products
  • Experience designing and evaluating A/B tests, including statistical significance
